   572→  const respondToQuestion = useCallback(
   573→    async (questionId: string, answers: string[], freeText?: string) => {
   574→      // Update the segment to show the user's answer
   575→      const segIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   576→        (s) => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData?.questionId === questionId
   577→      );
   578→      if (segIdx !== -1) {
   579→        segmentsRef.current[segIdx] = {
   580→          ...segmentsRef.current[segIdx],
   581→          userAnswer: answers,
   582→          userFreeText: freeText,
   583→        };
   584→        setMessages((prev) =>
   585→          prev.map((m) =>
   586→            m.id === assistantIdRef.current
   587→              ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   588→              : m
   589→          )
   590→        );
   591→      }
   592→
   593→      // POST to backend
   594→      try {
   595→        await fetch('/api/chat/respond', {
   596→          method: 'POST',
   597→          headers: {
   598→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   599→            ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   600→          },
   601→          body: JSON.stringify({
   602→            question_id: questionId,
   603→            answers,
   604→            free_text: freeText || null,
   605→          }),
   606→        });
   607→      } catch (e) {
   608→        console.error('Failed to respond to question:', e);
   609→      }
   610→    },
   611→    [userSessionId]
   612→  );
   613→
   614→  const loadMessages = useCallback((msgs: ChatMessage[], outgoingConversationId?: string | null, incomingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   615→    console.log('[loadMessages] outgoing:', outgoingConversationId, 'incoming:', incomingConversationId);
   616→    console.log('[loadMessages] API msgs:', msgs.length, msgs.map(m => ({role: m.role, contentLen: m.content?.length, segCount: m.segments?.length, segTypes: m.segments?.map(s => s.type)})));
   617→
   618→    // Abort any ongoing stream before loading a different conversation
   619→    if (abortRef.current) {
   620→      abortRef.current.abort();
   621→      abortRef.current = null;
   622→    }
   623→    if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   624→      clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   625→      flushTimerRef.current = null;
   626→    }
   627→
   628→    // Check cache for incoming conversation — use cached messages if they
   629→    // have more data than what the API returned (backend persistence may
   630→    // not have completed yet when the user switches back quickly).
   631→    let finalMsgs = msgs;
   632→    if (incomingConversationId) {
   633→      const cached = messagesCacheRef.current.get(incomingConversationId);
   634→      console.log('[loadMessages] cache for', incomingConversationId, ':', cached ? cached.length + ' msgs' : 'MISS', cached?.map(m => ({role: m.role, contentLen: m.content?.length, segCount: m.segments?.length, segTypes: m.segments?.map(s => s.type)})));
   635→      if (cached && cached.length > msgs.length) {
   636→        finalMsgs = cached;
   637→        console.log('[loadMessages] USING CACHE (cached:', cached.length, '> api:', msgs.length, ')');
   638→      } else {
   639→        console.log('[loadMessages] USING API (cached:', cached?.length, '<= api:', msgs.length, ')');
   640→      }
   641→      // Clear used cache entry so stale data doesn't persist forever
   642→      messagesCacheRef.current.delete(incomingConversationId);
   643→    }
   644→
   645→    // Save current messages to cache before replacing them.
   646→    // Uses functional setMessages to read the latest state without
   647→    // needing `messages` in the dependency array.
   648→    setMessages(prev => {
   649→      if (outgoingConversationId && prev.length > 0) {
   650→        // Flush any pending text buffer into the streaming assistant message
   651→        const pendingText = textBufferRef.current;
   652→        const aId = assistantIdRef.current;
   653→
   654→        // Build finalized segments including any unflushed text
   655→        let finalSegments = [...segmentsRef.current];
   656→        const pendingSegmentText = currentTextRef.current + pendingText;
   657→        if (pendingSegmentText.trim()) {
   658→          const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   659→          finalSegments.push({ type: segType as ContentSegment['type'], text: pendingSegmentText });
   660→        }
   661→
   662→        const cleaned = prev.map(m => {
   663→          if (m.id === aId && m.isStreaming) {
   664→            return {
   665→              ...m,
   666→              content: m.content + pendingText,
   667→              isStreaming: false,
   668→              currentPhase: undefined,
   669→              segments: finalSegments.length > 0 ? finalSegments : m.segments,
   670→            };
   671→          }
   672→          return m;
   673→        });
   674→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoingConversationId, cleaned);
   675→        console.log('[loadMessages] CACHED outgoing', outgoingConversationId, ':', cleaned.length, 'msgs', cleaned.map(m => ({role: m.role, contentLen: m.content?.length, segCount: m.segments?.length, segTypes: m.segments?.map(s => s.type)})));
   676→      }
   677→      console.log('[loadMessages] FINAL msgs to render:', finalMsgs.length, finalMsgs.map(m => ({role: m.role, contentLen: m.content?.length, segCount: m.segments?.length, isStreaming: m.isStreaming})));
   678→      return finalMsgs;
   679→    });
   680→
   681→    setIsStreaming(false);
   682→    textBufferRef.current = '';
   683→    currentTextRef.current = '';
   684→    segmentsRef.current = [];
   685→    assistantIdRef.current = '';
   686→    phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   687→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   688→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   689→  }, []);
   690→
   691→  const clearMessages = useCallback((outgoingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   692→    if (abortRef.current) {
   693→      abortRef.current.abort();
   694→    }
   695→    setMessages(prev => {
   696→      if (outgoingConversationId && prev.length > 0) {
   697→        const cleaned = prev.map(m =>
   698→          m.isStreaming ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined } : m
   699→        );
   700→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoingConversationId, cleaned);
   701→      }
   702→      return [];
   703→    });
   704→    setIsStreaming(false);
   705→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   706→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   707→  }, []);
   708→
   709→  return (
   710→    <AgentContext.Provider
   711→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, editMessage, deleteMessage, clearMessages, loadMessages, respondToQuestion }}
   712→    >
   713→      {children}
   714→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   715→  );
   716→}
   717→

    22→def build_system_prompt(db: Database) -> str:

    25→
    26→Tools at your disposal:
    27→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__execute_sql — run SQL queries against DuckDB
    28→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__render_chart — render a Plotly chart
    29→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__ask_user_question — ask the user a clarifying question
    30→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__create_skill — create a reusable skill (workflow template) that can be invoked later via /skill-name
    31→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__save_memory — save a fact, preference, or pattern to long-term memory
    32→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__recall_memories — retrieve stored memories
    33→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__forget_memory — remove a specific memory
    34→- Task tool with subagent_type "sql-analyst" — delegate complex multi-query data exploration
    35→
    36→Skill creation workflow (follow this exactly):
    37→When the user asks you to create a skill, do NOT call create_skill immediately. Follow this process:
    38→1. If the user's request is vague or unclear about what the skill should do, use ask_user_question to clarify the skill's purpose, scope, and key steps BEFORE drafting any plan. Keep asking until you have enough detail.
    39→2. Once the requirements are clear, draft a plan for the skill: propose a name, description, and outline of the content (step-by-step instructions).
    40→3. Present the plan to the user using ask_user_question with options like "Looks good, create it" and "I want to make changes". Describe the full plan in the question text.
    41→4. If the user wants changes, update the plan based on their feedback and present the revised plan again (repeat step 3).
    42→5. Only after the user explicitly approves the plan, call create_skill with the finalized name, description, and content.
    43→- NEVER skip the review step. The user must approve before the skill is created.
    44→
    45→Task tool usage:
    46→- When using the Task tool, set "subagent_type" to "sql-analyst". NEVER use generic values like "general-purpose" or any other value.
    47→- Do NOT set the "model" parameter on the Task tool — it is already configured.
    48→- After the sql-analyst returns, do NOT repeat the data tables, numbers, or SQL queries. Simply add brief commentary on what the data means. Never include SQL in your answer unless the user explicitly asks to see the SQL.
    49→
    50→Direct tool usage:
    51→- For simple SQL queries, call execute_sql directly instead of delegating to sql-analyst.
    52→- For charts/visualizations, call execute_sql to get the data, then call render_chart yourself with the Plotly spec. Do NOT delegate charting to a subagent.
    53→
    54→Charting workflow (follow this exactly):
    55→1. Run execute_sql to get the data you need for a chart.
    56→2. Call render_chart with TWO required parameters:
    57→   - `data`: array of Plotly trace objects (e.g. [{"type": "bar", "x": [...], "y": [...]}])
    58→   - `layout`: object that MUST include `title` (e.g. {"title": "My Chart"})
    59→   Both `data` and `layout` are required — the tool WILL accept both. Do not second-guess this.
    60→3. After the chart renders, write your narrative text discussing what the chart shows.
    61→4. Repeat steps 1-3 for each additional chart. This produces interleaved charts and narrative.
    62→- Do NOT render all charts first and then write all narrative at the end.
    63→- Do NOT output chart JSON as a code block. Always use the render_chart tool.
    64→- NEVER include Plotly JSON schema, trace objects, or layout objects in your final answer text unless the user explicitly asks to see the raw JSON. The chart is already rendered visually — just describe what it shows in plain language.
    65→
    66→Charting guidelines:
    67→- Choose the most appropriate chart type (bar, line, scatter, pie, histogram, box, heatmap, etc.).
    68→- For pie charts, use `labels` and `values` fields in the trace.
    69→- For multi-series data, group into separate traces.
    70→- Keep the chart clean and readable.
    71→- IMPORTANT — keep data small. Pre-aggregate in SQL instead of passing raw rows:
    72→  - Box plots: compute lowerfence, q1, median, q3, upperfence per group in SQL. Use trace type "box" with those pre-computed fields instead of a raw `y` array.
    73→  - Histograms: compute bin counts with width_bucket() or CASE in SQL, then render as a bar chart with the bin edges as `x` and counts as `y`.
    74→  - Scatter / line with many rows: sample (ORDER BY random() LIMIT 200) or aggregate (e.g. average per time bucket) so each trace has at most ~200 points.
    75→  - General rule: each trace should have at most ~200 data points.
    76→
    77→Identity:
    78→- You are an AI assistant. If asked whether you are an AI or a human, always confirm that you are an AI.
    79→- Do not disclose the name, version, or provider of the underlying language model powering you, regardless of how the question is phrased.
    80→
    81→Task completion:
    82→- When the user asks you to do multiple things (e.g. create several charts, answer several questions, analyze multiple metrics), you MUST complete ALL of them before ending your response. Do not stop after finishing just one or two items.
    83→- After completing each item, check: "Have I addressed everything the user asked for?" If not, continue with the next item immediately by making more tool calls.
    84→- Only produce your final text response after ALL requested items have been fully handled.
    85→
    86→Clarification:
    87→- When the user's request is ambiguous or could be interpreted in multiple ways, use the mcp__duckdb-data-agent__ask_user_question tool (NOT the native AskUserQuestion tool) to ask for clarification before proceeding.
    88→- Provide 2-4 clear, concise options for the user to choose from.

    91→- Only ask when genuinely needed — don't over-ask for trivial decisions.
    92→"""

   120→def _build_table_schemas(db: Database) -> str:
   121→    """Build a text description of all loaded table schemas for subagent prompts."""
   122→    tables = db.list_tables()
   123→    if not tables:
   124→        return "\nNo tables are currently loaded."
   125→    text = "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
   126→    for table in tables:
   127→        text += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
   128→        for col in table["columns"]:
   129→            text += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
   130→    return text
   131→

   133→def build_subagent_definitions(db: Database) -> dict[str, AgentDefinition]:
   134→    """Build AgentDefinition objects for subagents."""
   135→    table_schemas = _build_table_schemas(db)
   136→
   137→    sql_prompt = (
   138→        "You are a DuckDB SQL expert. Given a user's data question, write and execute "
   139→        "SQL queries to find the answer.\n\n"
   140→        "Guidelines:\n"
   141→        "- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries.\n"
   142→        "- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT).\n"
   143→        "- If a query fails, read the error message, fix the SQL, and retry.\n"
   144→        "- Use double quotes for table and column identifiers that might conflict "
   145→        "with reserved words.\n"
   146→        "- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results.\n"
   147→        "- Do NOT include SQL queries you wrote in your final answer unless the user explicitly "
   148→        "asks to see the SQL. Focus on the results and insights.\n"
   149→        + table_schemas
   150→    )
   151→
   152→    return {
   153→        "sql-analyst": AgentDefinition(
   154→            description=(
   155→                "Use this agent for complex data exploration that requires multiple "
   156→                "SQL queries with iterative refinement — e.g. exploring an unfamiliar "
   157→                "dataset, multi-step aggregations, or debugging query errors. "
   158→                "For simple one-off queries, call execute_sql directly instead."
   159→            ),
   160→            prompt=sql_prompt,
   161→            tools=["mcp__duckdb-data-agent__execute_sql"],
   162→            model=SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL_SDK,
   163→        ),
   164→    }

   167→def _build_message_with_history(
   168→    message: str, conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None
   169→) -> str:
   170→    """Prepend conversation history context to the user message when editing."""
   171→    if not conversation_history:
   172→        return message
   173→
   174→    history_text = "Previous conversation (for context, I am now editing a message):\n"
   175→    for entry in conversation_history:
   176→        role = entry.get("role", "user").capitalize()
   177→        content = entry.get("content", "")
   178→        history_text += f"\n{role}: {content}\n"
   179→    history_text += f"\n---\n\nMy updated message:\n{message}"
   180→    return history_text

   184→async def stream_chat(
   185→    message: str,
   186→    session_id: str | None = None,
   187→    db: Database | None = None,
   188→    conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None,
   189→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None,
   190→    backend_session_id: str | None = None,
   191→    skills: list[str] | None = None,
   192→    conversation_id: str | None = None,
   193→) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
   194→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events via containerized sidecar."""
   195→    # Persist user message if conversation_id provided
   196→    if conversation_id:
   197→        try:
   198→            memory_store.add_message(conversation_id, "user", message)
   199→        except Exception:
   200→            logger.warning("Failed to persist user message", exc_info=True)
   201→
   202→    from app.container_manager import container_manager
   203→    if container_manager is None:
   204→        raise RuntimeError("Docker is not available. Container mode requires Docker.")
   205→    if db is None:
   206→        raise ValueError("db must be provided")
   207→
   208→    import httpx
   209→    import asyncio
   210→
   211→    # Only bake history into message text for edit/delete (no session to resume).
   212→    # For normal follow-ups with a session_id, pass history as a separate field
   213→    # so the sidecar can use it as fallback if resume fails.
   214→    query_message = _build_message_with_history(message, conversation_history) if not session_id else message
   215→    system_prompt = build_system_prompt(db)
   216→
   217→    # Pass Langfuse credentials to the container so the sidecar's
   218→    # TypeScript Langfuse SDK can create traces directly.
   219→    env: dict[str, str] = {
   220→        "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "placeholder",
   221→        "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{BACKEND_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   222→        "SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS": str(SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS),
   223→    }
   224→    if LANGFUSE_ENABLED:
   225→        env["LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY
   226→        env["LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY
   227→        env["LANGFUSE_BASE_URL"] = LANGFUSE_BASE_URL
   228→
   229→    # Use the backend session ID (X-Session-ID header) for both:
   230→    # 1. MCP SSE URL — so the container queries the correct DuckDB instance
   231→    # 2. Container lifecycle key — so the same container is reused across
   232→    #    requests from the same browser tab (the Claude agent session_id
   233→    #    changes after the first response, which would orphan the container)
   234→    stable_session = backend_session_id or session_id or "default"
   235→
   236→    # Declare persistence variables before try so they're accessible in finally
   237→    assistant_text_parts: list[str] = []
   238→    all_text_parts: list[str] = []
   239→    assistant_metadata: dict = {"sql_queries": [], "chart_specs": []}
   240→    persisted_segments: list[dict] = []
   241→    seg_thinking_buf: list[str] = []
   242→    seg_answer_buf: list[str] = []
   243→    seg_phase = "thinking"
   244→    actual_session_id = session_id
   245→
   246→    try:
   247→        # Send SSE keepalive immediately so the HTTP response starts and
   248→        # intermediate proxies (Vite, nginx) don't drop the idle connection
   249→        # before we've finished the blocking Docker container creation.
   250→        yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   251→
   252→        # container_manager.create() is synchronous (blocking Docker API call).
   253→        # Run it in a thread executor so the event loop stays responsive and
   254→        # can continue flushing keepalives to the client during startup.
   255→        # gVisor (runsc) containers can take 10-30 seconds to spin up.
   256→        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   257→        create_future = loop.run_in_executor(None, container_manager.create, stable_session, env)
   258→
   259→        max_create_wait = 60.0
   260→        elapsed = 0.0
   261→        while not create_future.done():
   262→            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
   263→            elapsed += 2.0
   264→            if elapsed >= max_create_wait:
   265→                create_future.cancel()
   266→                raise RuntimeError(f"Container creation timed out after {max_create_wait:.0f}s")
   267→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   268→        info = await create_future
   269→        container_manager.touch(stable_session)
   270→
   271→        # Wait for container to be ready
   272→        for attempt in range(10):
   273→            try:
   274→                async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(5.0)) as check_client:
   275→                    resp = await check_client.get(f"{info.url}/health")
   276→                    if resp.status_code == 200:
   277→                        break
   278→            except Exception:
   279→                pass
   280→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   281→            await asyncio.sleep(1)
   282→        else:
   283→            raise RuntimeError("Sidecar container failed health check after 10 attempts")
   284→

   326→        # Track subagent text output from intermediate assistant messages.

   328→        # usage), not the subagent's actual output.  The real output arrives in
   329→        # assistant messages whose parent_tool_use_id matches the Task tool ID.
   330→        subagent_texts: dict[str, str] = {}
   331→        # Track SQL queries executed inside subagents so they can be
   332→        # included in the subagent_end event for visibility.
   333→        subagent_sql_data: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}  # parent_tool_use_id -> [{tool_id, sql, columns?, rows?, rowCount?}]
   334→        subagent_internal_tools: dict[str, str] = {}   # tool_id -> parent_tool_use_id
   335→
   336→        # Timeout must exceed SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS so the sidecar's own
   337→        # idle abort fires first; add a 60s buffer.
   338→        httpx_timeout = (SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000) + 60
   339→        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(httpx_timeout)) as client:
   340→            async with client.stream("POST", f"{info.url}/query", json=payload) as response:
   341→                line_iter = response.aiter_lines().__aiter__()
   342→                while True:
   343→                    try:
   344→                        if waiting_for_user:
   345→                            try:
   346→                                line = await asyncio.wait_for(line_iter.__anext__(), timeout=5.0)
   347→                            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   348→                                yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   349→                                continue
   350→                        else:
   351→                            line = await line_iter.__anext__()
   352→                    except StopAsyncIteration:
   353→                        break
   354→                    except httpx.RemoteProtocolError:
   355→                        # Sidecar closed the connection before sending a
   356→                        # proper chunked-encoding terminator.  This is
   357→                        # benign — the sidecar's Express handler calls
   358→                        # res.end() in its finally block and the
   359→                        # connection can tear down before the last chunk
   360→                        # delimiter reaches us.  Treat it as end-of-stream.
   361→                        logger.warning("Sidecar closed connection early (incomplete chunked read); treating as end-of-stream")
   362→                        break

   365→                        continue

   367→                    waiting_for_user = False
   368→                    try:
   369→                        msg = json.loads(raw)
   370→                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
   371→                        continue
   372→

   374→                    # long-running queries don't trigger the reaper.
   375→                    container_manager.touch(stable_session)
   376→
   377→                    msg_type = msg.get("type")

   379→                    if msg_type == "stream_event":
   380→                        event = msg.get("event", {})
   381→                        event_type = event.get("type", "")
   382→                        stream_parent = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
   383→                        is_subagent_event = bool(stream_parent and stream_parent in tool_names)
   384→
   385→                        if event_type == "content_block_delta":
   386→                            delta = event.get("delta", {})
   387→                            delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
   388→                            if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
   389→                                text = delta.get("thinking", "")
   390→                                if text and not is_subagent_event:
   391→                                    seg_thinking_buf.append(text)
   392→                                    yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   393→                            elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   394→                                text = delta.get("text", "")
   395→                                if text:
   396→                                    # Accumulate subagent text for chart_spec
   397→                                    # extraction (the SDK may not yield a
   398→                                    # complete assistant message for the
   399→                                    # subagent's final turn).
   400→                                    if is_subagent_event:
   401→                                        subagent_texts[stream_parent] = subagent_texts.get(stream_parent, "") + text
   402→                                    else:
   403→                                        event_name = "answer" if has_tool_calls else "thinking"
   404→                                        all_text_parts.append(text)
   405→                                        # Track text in segment buffers based on actual phase
   406→                                        if seg_phase == "thinking":
   407→                                            seg_thinking_buf.append(text)
   408→                                        else:
   409→                                            seg_answer_buf.append(text)
   410→                                        if event_name == "answer":
   411→                                            assistant_text_parts.append(text)

   413→
   414→                        elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   415→                            block = event.get("content_block", {})
   416→                            block_type = block.get("type")
   417→                            if block_type == "thinking":
   418→                                if not is_subagent_event:
   419→                                    has_thinking = True
   420→                            elif block_type == "text":
   421→                                if has_thinking and not is_subagent_event:
   422→                                    # Flush thinking buffer into a segment
   423→                                    if seg_thinking_buf:
   424→                                        persisted_segments.append({"type": "thinking", "text": "".join(seg_thinking_buf)})
   425→                                        seg_thinking_buf.clear()
   426→                                    seg_phase = "answer"

   428→                            elif block_type == "tool_use":

   453→

   576→                            name = tool_names.get(tool_id, "")

   585→
   586→                            # Try to parse structured MCP result

   601→                                elif parsed.get("status") == "error":

   662→
   663→                    # --- Final result ---
   664→                    elif msg_type == "result":
   665→                        actual_session_id = msg.get("session_id") or actual_session_id
   666→                        if msg.get("is_error"):
   667→                            errors = msg.get("errors", [])
   668→                            error_text = msg.get("result") or "; ".join(errors) or "Unknown error"
   669→                            yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   670→                        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   671→                        done_sent = True
   672→
   673→                    # --- Sidecar error (e.g. SDK/CLI crash inside container) ---
   674→                    elif msg_type == "error":
   675→                        error_text = msg.get("message") or "Sidecar error"
   676→                        logger.error("Sidecar reported error: %s", error_text)
   677→                        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   678→                        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   679→                        done_sent = True
   680→
   681→                    # --- Extract session_id early from system init ---
   682→                    elif msg_type == "system":
   683→                        sys_session = msg.get("session_id")
   684→                        if sys_session:
   685→                            actual_session_id = sys_session

   687→        # Guard: always send done even if sidecar ended without result message
   688→        if not done_sent:
   689→            logger.warning("Sidecar stream ended without result message; sending done event")
   690→            yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': 'Connection to agent was lost. Please try again.'})}\n\n"
   691→            yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   692→
   693→    except Exception as e:
   694→        logger.error("Container agent error: %s", str(e))
   695→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   696→    finally:
   697→        # Persist assistant message even when the client disconnects

   699→        # the assistant response would be lost on reload.
   700→        text_to_persist = assistant_text_parts or all_text_parts
   701→        if conversation_id and text_to_persist:

   714→                    persisted_segments.append({"type": "answer", "text": "".join(all_text_parts)})

   716→                meta = assistant_metadata.copy() if (assistant_metadata["sql_queries"] or assistant_metadata["chart_specs"]) else {}
   717→                if persisted_segments:
   718→                    meta["segments"] = persisted_segments
   719→                memory_store.add_message(
   720→                    conversation_id, "assistant", "".join(text_to_persist),
   721→                    metadata=meta if meta else None,
   722→                )
   723→            except Exception:
   724→                logger.warning("Failed to persist assistant message", exc_info=True)
   725→    # Note: Container is kept alive for session resume (--resume flag) and is
   726→    # cleaned up by the background cleanup loop after
   727→    # CONTAINER_MAX_LIFETIME_SECONDS, or on application shutdown.

     1→"""SQLite-backed memory store for conversations and messages."""
     2→
     3→import json
     4→import os
     5→import sqlite3
     6→import threading
     7→import uuid
     8→from datetime import datetime, timezone
     9→
    10→from app.config import MEMORY_DB_PATH
    11→
    12→
    13→def _now_iso() -> str:
    14→    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    15→
    16→
    17→class MemoryStore:
    18→    """Thread-safe SQLite store for conversation history.
    19→
    20→    Uses WAL mode for concurrent reads, a threading.Lock for write
    21→    serialisation, and CASCADE deletes on messages.
    22→    """
    23→
    24→    def __init__(self, db_path: str = MEMORY_DB_PATH) -> None:
    25→        self._db_path = db_path
    26→        self._lock = threading.Lock()
    27→        self._ensure_dir()
    28→        self._init_schema()
    29→
    30→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→    # Internal helpers
    32→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→
    34→    def _ensure_dir(self) -> None:
    35→        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self._db_path), exist_ok=True)
    36→
    37→    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    38→        conn = sqlite3.connect(self._db_path)
    39→        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    40→        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
    41→        return conn
    42→
    43→    def _init_schema(self) -> None:
    44→        with self._lock:
    45→            conn = self._connect()
    46→            try:
    47→                conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
    48→                conn.executescript(
    49→                    """
    50→                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS conversations (
    51→                        id         TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    52→                        user_id    TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'default',
    53→                        title      TEXT,
    54→                        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    55→                        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
    56→                    );
    57→
    58→                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
    59→                        id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    60→                        conversation_id TEXT NOT NULL
    61→                            REFERENCES conversations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    62→                        role            TEXT NOT NULL,
    63→                        content         TEXT NOT NULL,
    64→                        metadata        TEXT,
    65→                        created_at      TEXT NOT NULL,
    66→                        sort_order      INTEGER NOT NULL
    67→                    );
    68→
    69→                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_conv_order
    70→                        ON messages(conversation_id, sort_order);
    71→
    72→                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_conversations_user_updated
    73→                        ON conversations(user_id, updated_at);
    74→                    """
    75→                )
    76→                conn.commit()
    77→            finally:
    78→                conn.close()
    79→
    80→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    81→    # Conversations
    82→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    83→
    84→    def create_conversation(
    85→        self, user_id: str = "default", title: str | None = None
    86→    ) -> dict:
    87→        now = _now_iso()
    88→        conv_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    89→        row = {
    90→            "id": conv_id,
    91→            "user_id": user_id,
    92→            "title": title,
    93→            "created_at": now,
    94→            "updated_at": now,
    95→        }
    96→        with self._lock:
    97→            conn = self._connect()
    98→            try:
    99→                conn.execute(
   100→                    """
   101→                    INSERT INTO conversations (id, user_id, title, created_at, updated_at)
   102→                    VALUES (:id, :user_id, :title, :created_at, :updated_at)
   103→                    """,
   104→                    row,
   105→                )
   106→                conn.commit()
   107→            finally:
   108→                conn.close()
   109→        return row
   110→
   111→    def list_conversations(
   112→        self,
   113→        user_id: str = "default",
   114→        limit: int = 50,
   115→        offset: int = 0,
   116→    ) -> list[dict]:
   117→        with self._lock:
   118→            conn = self._connect()
   119→            try:
   120→                rows = conn.execute(
   121→                    """
   122→                    SELECT * FROM conversations
   123→                    WHERE user_id = ?
   124→                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
   125→                    LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
   126→                    """,
   127→                    (user_id, limit, offset),
   128→                ).fetchall()
   129→            finally:
   130→                conn.close()
   131→        return [dict(r) for r in rows]
   132→
   133→    def get_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> dict | None:
   134→        with self._lock:
   135→            conn = self._connect()
   136→            try:
   137→                row = conn.execute(
   138→                    "SELECT * FROM conversations WHERE id = ?",
   139→                    (conversation_id,),
   140→                ).fetchone()
   141→                if row is None:
   142→                    return None
   143→                conv = dict(row)
   144→                msgs = conn.execute(
   145→                    """
   146→                    SELECT * FROM messages
   147→                    WHERE conversation_id = ?
   148→                    ORDER BY sort_order
   149→                    """,
   150→                    (conversation_id,),
   151→                ).fetchall()
   152→            finally:
   153→                conn.close()
   154→        conv["messages"] = [dict(m) for m in msgs]
   155→        return conv
   156→
   157→    def update_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, title: str) -> bool:
   158→        with self._lock:
   159→            conn = self._connect()
   160→            try:
   161→                cur = conn.execute(
   162→                    """
   163→                    UPDATE conversations
   164→                    SET title = ?, updated_at = ?
   165→                    WHERE id = ?
   166→                    """,
   167→                    (title, _now_iso(), conversation_id),
   168→                )
   169→                conn.commit()
   170→                changed = cur.rowcount > 0
   171→            finally:
   172→                conn.close()
   173→        return changed
   174→
   175→    def delete_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> bool:
   176→        with self._lock:
   177→            conn = self._connect()
   178→            try:
   179→                cur = conn.execute(
   180→                    "DELETE FROM conversations WHERE id = ?",
   181→                    (conversation_id,),
   182→                )
   183→                conn.commit()
   184→                changed = cur.rowcount > 0
   185→            finally:
   186→                conn.close()
   187→        return changed
   188→
   189→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   190→    # Messages
   191→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   192→
   193→    def add_message(
   194→        self,

   196→        role: str,

   202→        meta_json = json.dumps(metadata) if metadata is not None else None
   203→
   204→        with self._lock:

   206→            try:
   207→                # Determine next sort_order for this conversation.

   209→                    """

   211→                    FROM messages
   212→                    WHERE conversation_id = ?
   213→                    """,
   214→                    (conversation_id,),
   215→                ).fetchone()
   216→                next_order = row["next_order"]
   217→
   218→                conn.execute(

   222→                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
   223→                    """,
   224→                    (msg_id, conversation_id, role, content, meta_json, now, next_order),
   225→                )
   226→                # Touch the conversation's updated_at timestamp.
   227→                conn.execute(
   228→                    "UPDATE conversations SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   229→                    (now, conversation_id),
   230→                )
   231→                conn.commit()
   232→            finally:
   233→                conn.close()
   234→
   235→        return {

   241→            "created_at": now,
   242→            "sort_order": next_order,

   244→
   245→    def list_messages(self, conversation_id: str) -> list[dict]:

   253→                    ORDER BY sort_order
   254→                    """,
   255→                    (conversation_id,),
   256→                ).fetchall()
   257→            finally:
   258→                conn.close()
   259→        return [dict(r) for r in rows]

> AGENT

Now I have the full picture. Let me follow the systematic debugging approach and trace the exact data flow.

**Root cause analysis** (Phase 1 complete):

The bug is in `loadMessages` cache comparison logic in `AgentContext.tsx` line 635:
```typescript
if (cached && cached.length > msgs.length) {
```

When switching A→B→A:
1. Switch A→B: caches A's messages (2 msgs: user + assistant)
2. Switch B→A: fetches A from API (also 2 msgs if persistence succeeded)
3. `cached.length (2) > msgs.length (2)` → **FALSE** → cache ignored, API used

The API messages may have inferior segment data (serialized/deserialized from DB) vs the rich in-memory cached segments. Worse, if persistence raced with the fetch, the API assistant message could have incomplete or empty content.

Additionally, `clearMessages` (new-conversation path) doesn't flush `textBufferRef`/`segmentsRef` into cached messages, so that path also loses data.

Let me fix both issues:

    18→function stripChartSpecBlocks(text: string): string {
    19→  return text.replace(/```(?:json)?\s*\n?\s*\{[\s\S]*?"(?:chart_spec|data)"[\s\S]*?\}\s*\n?\s*```/g, '').trim();

    22→/**

    26→ * paragraph breaks so they render on new lines.
    27→ */
    28→function fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(text: string): string {
    29→  // Insert \n\n before **Header** when preceded by sentence-ending punctuation
    30→  // with no whitespace. The [A-Z] ensures we only match section-header-style
    31→  // bold (starting with a capital letter), avoiding inline bold like **data**.
    32→  return text.replace(/([.!?:])(\*\*[A-Z])/g, '$1\n\n$2');

    36→function stripMarkdown(text: string): string {

    39→    .replace(/__(.+?)__/g, '$1')        // __bold__
    40→    .replace(/\*(.+?)\*/g, '$1')        // *italic*
    41→    .replace(/_(.+?)_/g, '$1')          // _italic_
    42→    .replace(/`(.+?)`/g, '$1')          // `code`

   353→          {questionSegments.map((seg, i) => (
   354→            <div key={`question-${i}`} className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--question">
   355→              <UserQuestion
   356→                questionData={seg.questionData!}
   357→                userAnswer={seg.userAnswer}
   358→                userFreeText={seg.userFreeText}
   359→              />
   360→            </div>
   361→          ))}
   362→          {answerBlockSegments.length > 0 && (
   363→            <div className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--answer">
   364→              <div className="message-bubble__segment-label message-bubble__segment-label--answer">{t('answer')}</div>
   365→              {answerBlockSegments.map((seg, i) => {
   366→                // Chart segment (tool with chart_spec or subagent_end with chart_spec)
   367→                if ((seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult?.chart_spec) || (seg.type === 'subagent_end' && seg.chart_spec)) {
   368→                  return (
   369→                    <div key={`chart-${i}`} className="message-bubble__chart-in-answer">
   370→                      {seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult ? (
   371→                        <InlineQueryResult result={seg.toolResult!} />
   372→                      ) : seg.chart_spec ? (
   373→                        <ChartWidget data={seg.chart_spec.data} layout={seg.chart_spec.layout} frames={seg.chart_spec.frames} />
   374→                      ) : null}
   375→                    </div>
   376→                  );
   377→                }
   378→                // Text segment (answer or subagent_end without chart_spec)
   379→                return (
   380→                  <div key={`answer-${i}`} className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   381→                    <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(seg.text!)}</ReactMarkdown>
   382→                  </div>
   383→                );
   384→              })}
   385→              {/* Render streaming remainder inside the same answer block to avoid duplicate headers */}
   386→              {isInAnswerPhase && streamingRemainder?.trim() && (() => {
   387→                const displayText = hasCharts ? stripChartSpecBlocks(streamingRemainder) : streamingRemainder;
   388→                return displayText?.trim() ? (
   389→                  <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   390→                    <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(displayText)}</ReactMarkdown>
   391→                  </div>
   392→                ) : null;
   393→              })()}
   394→            </div>
   395→          )}
   396→          {/* Only show standalone streaming answer block when no answer segments exist yet */}
   397→          {answerBlockSegments.length === 0 && isInAnswerPhase && streamingRemainder?.trim() && (() => {
   398→            const displayText = hasCharts ? stripChartSpecBlocks(streamingRemainder) : streamingRemainder;
   399→            return displayText?.trim() ? (
   400→              <div className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--answer">
   401→                <div className="message-bubble__segment-label message-bubble__segment-label--answer">{t('answer')}</div>
   402→                <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   403→                  <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(displayText)}</ReactMarkdown>
   404→                </div>
   405→              </div>
   406→            ) : null;
   407→          })()}
   408→          {message.segments!.filter((s) => s.type === 'error').map((seg, i) => {
   409→            const lastUserMsg = messages.slice().reverse().find((m) => m.role === 'user');
   410→            const handleRetry = lastUserMsg ? () => sendMessage(lastUserMsg.content) : undefined;
   411→            return <ErrorBlock key={`error-${i}`} errorMessage={seg.errorMessage || 'Unknown error'} onRetry={!isStreaming ? handleRetry : undefined} />;
   412→          })}
   413→          {message.isStreaming && !message.content && questionSegments.length === 0 && (
   414→            <span className="message-bubble__typing">{t('thinking')}</span>
   415→          )}
   416→        </div>
   417→      ) : (
   418→        <>
   419→          <div className="message-bubble__content">
   420→            {message.content ? (
   421→              <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(message.content)}</ReactMarkdown>
   422→            ) : message.isStreaming ? (
   423→              <span className="message-bubble__typing">{t('thinking')}</span>
   424→            ) : null}
   425→          </div>
   426→          {message.toolCalls && message.toolCalls.length > 0 && (
   427→            <div className="message-bubble__tools">
   428→              {message.toolCalls.map((tc) => (
   429→                <InlineQueryResult key={tc.toolCallId} result={tc} />
   430→              ))}
   431→            </div>
   432→          )}
   433→        </>
   434→      )}
   435→    </div>
   436→  );
   437→}
   438→

Now I have the full picture. Let me implement the fix.

**Root cause confirmed**: In `AgentContext.tsx` line 635, the cache comparison uses `>` (strict greater-than). When both cache and API have the same message count (the common case for completed responses), the cache is skipped and inferior API data is used. Additionally, `clearMessages` doesn't flush streaming refs before caching.

> AGENT

No type errors. Let me do a quick summary of the changes:

**Root cause**: The cache comparison in `loadMessages` used `cached.length > msgs.length` (strict greater-than). When both the cache and API had the same number of messages (the common case for completed responses), the cache was skipped and API messages were used instead. API messages could be incomplete (persistence race) or have inferior segment data (reconstructed from serialized JSON vs rich in-memory objects).

**Fixes applied** (2 changes in `AgentContext.tsx`):

1. **`loadMessages` cache logic** (line 632): Changed from `cached.length > msgs.length` to `cached.length > 0`. The in-memory cache always has the freshest, most complete data — prefer it whenever available.

2. **`clearMessages` ref flushing** (lines 683-728): Previously only set `isStreaming: false` on cached messages without flushing `textBufferRef`, `segmentsRef`, `currentTextRef`, or `phaseRef`. Now performs the same full flush as `loadMessages`, ensuring mid-stream messages are properly finalized before caching. Also resets all streaming refs afterward to prevent stale state.

3. **Cleaned up** debug `console.log` statements from both `AgentContext.tsx` and `ConversationContext.tsx`.
